# The three subsystems the decomp track scopes this project to, one function
# per line. Nothing outside this file is meant to be matched; the rest of the
# 14,620 functions stay reference assembly. See the track note for why.
#
# RENAMED 2026-08-22. The 42 functions in this file now carry descriptive
# PascalCase names instead of Ghidra's FUN_<addr>. The addresses are unchanged
# and still live in config/main.symbols.txt, which is the mapping back to a TU
# under recomp/output9 -- match on the ADDRESS there, not on the name.
#
# The names are INVENTED, not recovered: the ELF is stripped and the only real
# symbols in it are Metrowerks' own std:: strings. The one exception is the
# SB2 opcode set, which the interpreter prints in its own error messages -- see
# enum Opcode in src/event/ScriptVmExec_173b00.cpp, where ten entries are the binary's.
# --- 1. Animation clip evaluation and the clip clock -------------------------
# The clock that turned out to round where the EE truncates, plus the keyframe
# path around it. AnimKeyFindSpan_155ff0 carries the cvt.w.s the backlog names.
#               # NEEDS VU0 MACRO-MODE INTRINSICS, like QuatSlerp_13c270 -- it decodes
#               # keys with lqc2/vitof15.xyzw/vmul.xyzw/sqc2. Read but not drafted.
# --- 2. The SB2 event script VM ----------------------------------------------
# The interpreter and its two adjacent command tables. Watching the wrong table
# once made a busy script look deadlocked, so both dispatchers are in scope.
# EXCLUDED 2026-08-21. FUN_00134dc0 is not a magic check -- it is memcmp, and a
# hand-optimised one: pcpyld/psubw/pcpyud over 16-byte blocks with the 0x01010101
# and 0x80808080 word-at-a-time byte-test constants, plus alignment and length
# prologues. Library code, not one of the three subsystems, and almost certainly
# not reachable from C in the first place. ScriptVmInit_176af0 calls it as
# memcmp(blob, "SB2", 3) and that is all the track needs to know.
# FUN_00134dc0
# --- 3. The camera boom and the two camera stacks ----------------------------
# Including the keyframe evaluator that installs the cinematic pose and the
# mode dispatch that picks the town's driver.
# =============================================================================
# SCOPE EXPANSION, 2026-08-22 (second session of the day, run alongside another)
# =============================================================================
# The original 42 were three subsystems chosen as a research instrument. Under
# the FULL-decomp goal the scope is meant to grow, and these 31 are the first
# ring outward, picked by scripts/candidates.py rather than by hand -- it ranks
# every unregistered function by words, COP2 content, jump tables and varargs
# (what a match costs) against how many of the in-scope 42 call it and whether
# it carries a name RECOVERED from retail's own symbol table (what it is worth).
#
# 18 of the 31 are BYTE-IDENTICAL. Scores below are bytes, from
# scripts/bytecheck.py, taken with scripts/sandbox_build.py so that nothing in
# the shared build/obj or objdiff.json was touched while the other session ran.
#
# ÃƒÂ¢Ã…Â¡Ã‚Â  These keep their FUN_<addr> / retail-library names on purpose. Renaming
# means re-splitting, and .splache is shared -- see the handoff in README.md.
# --- 4. The script VM's native-call marshalling -------------------------------
# The {type, value} slot accessors every event command marshals through. They
# are what EvCmdEventGetTick_248fe0 and EvCmdEventSetTick_248fb0 already call, and they confirm
# ScriptVmExec_173b00's Slot tags (0 int, 1 float, 2 code, 3 reference) from a second TU.
# --- 5. World-object records: the ObjRecLookup_1c3ef0 family -------------------------
# A three-way table pick plus the flag pokes around it. The three bounds-checked
# lookups share one shape and one 8-word residue; it is written up in each file.
# --- 6. Small camera / world helpers -----------------------------------------
# --- 7. Library / CRT, names RECOVERED from retail's symbol table -------------
# full-decomp-plan Phase 3 says library first: the upstream source is public,
# so these cost the least per hour and they prove the flag model on code that
# was never ours. See config/libnames.txt for each name's provenance.
# --- Tier-1 ring picks, third session ------------------------------------
# Callees of functions already in scope, taken off config/queue.txt.
# --- Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session ------------------------------------
# Callees of in-scope functions, taken off a regenerated config/queue.txt.
# --- Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, second batch -----------------------
# --- Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, third batch ------------------------
# Seven VU0 macro-mode routines (inline asm -- COP2 is not expressible in C)
# and five more members of the asymmetric-guard-pair class.
# --- Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, fourth batch -----------------------
# --- Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, fifth batch ------------------------
# --- Tier-1 ring picks, fourth session, sixth batch ------------------------
# ==========================================================================
# MERGED 2026-08-23 from config/pending/w1.txt -- both w1 worker sessions.
# 3,489 functions, every one scored byte-identical or noted below, written
# under src/accessors, src/leaves, src/leaves2, src/leaves3, src/wrappers,
# src/wrappers2 and src/bodies.  See the fragment's own header for how they
# were produced and [[decomp-unit-generators]] for the toolchain.
# ==========================================================================
